## Next session: finish the remaining 29 books

Owner direction (2026-10-21): the supplied-PDF project is finished; the next session is to complete the remaining books through direct research. No additional PDFs will be provided or required. Begin with 1 Thessalonians, 2 Thessalonians and 1 John, then work through the 26 remaining Old Testament books in canonical order. The owner has authorized completion of this scope, including the established verified-batch publication workflow. This session records the handoff and commits the accumulated work; it does not begin the new editorial queue.

Read [the remaining-books plan](../planning/2026-10-21-remaining-bible-books.md) first, then the passage editorial and content execution standards. Preserve scholarly care in ordinary language, directly verify BSB text and source claims, actively investigate supported connections to God’s plan, and never originate a site position. Preserve existing URLs, related-study links, overlapping references and unrelated published prose. Continue with checked batches; a batch checkpoint is not completion of the whole queue. Record genuine interruptions and the next concrete passage in the plan.

The current completed baseline is 37 books / 2,429 reviewed groups, published and inventoried. The historical checkpoints below describe earlier stages and must not be mistaken for current pending work. The app goal controller was previously paused and has not been changed.

## Completed PDF-backed passage project

Owner authorization (2026-10-18): finish Genesis, then every New Testament book covered by the supplied PDFs, then Exodus and the remaining covered Old Testament books. Stay within the PDF-covered books; revisit uncovered books only after this work. Complete coherent passage coverage where a covered book currently has only scattered references. Review existing overlapping reference pages consistently and preserve their URLs. Publish verified batches as work proceeds.

**Telegram normal mode is active.** The owner-authorized PDF-backed passage queue is complete and published on2026-10-21: all37 covered books and2,429 reviewed passage groups. Genesis, all24 covered New Testament books and all12 remaining covered Old Testament books have finished coverage. The final Daniel and Jonah releases passed full-book BSB, coverage, rendering, navigation and live checks. No deployment is pending.
